Entry73/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 245
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from Sulaym b. Hanzala, from 'Umar b. al-Khattab:

Matn

that he used to say: “O God, I seek refuge in You from Your seizing me unawares, or leaving me in heedlessness, or making me among the heedless.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ سُلَيْمِ بْنِ حَنْظَلَةَ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ: «اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ أَنْ تَأْخُذَنِي عَلَى غِرَّةٍ، أَوْ أَنْ تَذَرَنِي فِي غَفْلَةٍ، أَوْ تَجْعَلَنِي مِنَ الْغَافِلِينَ»

Entry74/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 245
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from 'Uthman, from Sulayman b. Buraida, from his father, who said: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Whoever swears by trust (al-amana) is not of us; and whoever deceives a Muslim man about his family or his servant is not of us.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ بُرَيْدَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

مَنْ حَلَفَ بِالْأَمَانَةِ فَلَيْسَ مِنَّا، وَمَنْ غَشَّ امْرَأً مُسْلِمًا فِي أَهْلِهِ ، أَوْ خَادِمِهِ ، فَلَيْسَ مِنَّا»

Entry74b/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 246
English
Isnad

[Continuing, without a new chain:] And the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Whoever says, when he enters the evening and the morning: O God, You are my Lord; there is no god but You; You created me and I am Your servant; I am upon Your covenant as far as I am able; I acknowledge Your favor upon me and acknowledge my sin, so forgive me, for none forgives sins but You. If he dies that day, he dies a martyr, and if he dies that night, he dies a martyr.”

العربية
Isnad

وَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

مَنْ قَالَ إِذَا أَمْسَى وَأَصْبَحَ: اللَّهُمَّ أَنْتَ رَبِّي ، لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا أَنْتَ ، خَلَقْتَنِي وَأَنَا عَبْدُكَ، وَأَنَا عَلَى عَهْدِكَ مَا اسْتَطَعْتُ، أَبُوءُ بِنِعْمَتِكَ، وَأَبُوءُ بِذَنْبِي ، فَاغْفِرْ لِي، إِنَّهُ لَا يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلَّا أَنْتَ. فَإِنْ مَاتَ مِنْ يَوْمِهِ مَاتَ شَهِيدًا، وَإِنْ مَاتَ مِنْ لَيْلَتِهِ مَاتَ شَهِيدًا»

Entry75/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 246-248
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from a man, from 'Umar, who said: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“O 'Umar, say: O God, keep me with Islam standing; keep me with Islam sitting; keep me with Islam lying down; do not let an envious enemy prevail over me; and I ask You of the good, all of which is in Your hand, and I seek refuge in You from the evil of everything whose forelock You hold.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، عَنْ عُمَرَ، قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

يَا عُمَرُ قُلِ: اللَّهُمَّ احْفَظْنِي بِالْإِسْلَامِ قَائِمًا، وَاحْفَظْنِي بِالْإِسْلَامِ قَاعِدًا، وَاحْفَظْنِي بِالْإِسْلَامِ رَاقِدًا، وَلَا تُطِعْ فِيَّ عَدُوًّا حَاسِدًا، وَأَسْأَلُكَ مِنَ الْخَيْرِ الَّذِي كُلُّهُ بِيَدِكَ، وَأَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ ، أَنْتَ آخِذٌ بِنَاصِيَتِهِ»

Entry76/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 249-250
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Ibn Abi Khalid narrated to us, from Firas, from 'Amir, who said:

Matn

A group passed in the Jahiliyya by a man, who said: “Where are you heading?” They said: “Toward Bahrayn.” He said: “Shall I not send my son with you? You will buy for him, and he will learn from you, serve you and herd for you?” They said: “Yes.” So he sent him with them until they arrived in Bahrayn and stayed with a monk. When he entered his chapel he would bring the lamp, and the first thing he said on entering was: “In Your name, O God, You are the Compassionate, the Merciful, besides whom there is no god; the Originator, before whom there was nothing; the Everlasting, never heedless; the Living, who does not die; every day You are in a task; the Creator of what is seen and what is not seen; who taught everything without being taught.” The man said to the monk: “I have never seen a man with a more beautiful description of his God than you.” He said: So he learned his words. His companions who were with him said: “The son of so-and-so, we hear him speaking words we do not know what they are.” So they bound him and brought him to his father. He said to them: “What is the matter with my son?” They said: “He went mad after you left; he speaks words we do not understand.” His mother came and touched his body and said: “No, by Him by whom one swears, no jinn has entered my son's body.” They asked him, and he told them the story and the words he had learned from the man. His father said: “By God, we hear only good from you.” So they released him.

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ أَبِي خَالِدٍ، عَنْ فِرَاسٍ، عَنْ عَامِرٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

مَرَّ قَوْمٌ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ عَلَى رَجُلٍ ، فَقَالَ: أَيْنَ تَعْمِدُونَ؟ قَالُوا: نَحْوَ الْبَحْرَيْنِ. قَالَ: أَفَلَا أَبْعَثُ مَعَكُمُ ابْنِي هَذَا تَبْتَاعُونَ لَهُ، وَيَتَعَلَّمُ مِنْكُمْ وَيَخْدِمُكُمْ، وَيَرْعَى لَكُمْ؟ قَالُوا: نَعَمْ، فَأَرْسَلَهُ مَعَهُمْ حَتَّى قَدِمُوا الْبَحْرَيْنَ، فَنَزَلُوا بِرَجُلٍ رَاهِبٍ، فَكَانَ إِذَا دَخَلَ بَيْعَتَهُ جَاءَ بِالسِّرَاجِ، فَإِذَا دَخَلَ ، فَأَوَّلُ شَيْءٍ يَقُولُهُ: بِاسْمِكَ اللَّهُمَّ ، أَنْتَ الرَّحْمَنُ الرَّحِيمُ ، الَّذِي لَيْسَ إِلَهٌ غَيْرَكَ، الْبَدِيعُ الَّذِي لَيْسَ قَبْلَكَ شَيْءٌ ، الدَّائِمُ غَيْرُ الْغَافِلِ، الْحَيُّ الَّذِي لَا يَمُوتُ، كُلَّ يَوْمٍ أَنْتَ فِي شَأْنٍ، وَالْخَالِقُ مَا يُرَى ، وَمَا لَا يُرَى ، الَّذِي عَلِمَ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ بِغَيْرِ تَعْلِيمٍ» . فَقَالَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّاهِبِ: مَا رَأَيْتُ رَجُلًا أَحْسَنَ نَعْتًا مِنْ إِلَهِهِ مِنْكَ. قَالَ: فَتَعَلَّمَ كَلَامَهُ، فَقَالَ أَصْحَابُهُ الَّذِينَ مَعَهُ: إِنَّ ابْنَ فُلَانٍ نَسْمَعُهُ يَتَكَلَّمُ بِكَلَامٍ ، مَا نَدْرِي مَا هُوَ فَقَيَّدُوهُ، ثُمَّ أَتَوْا بِهِ أَبَاهُ، فَقَالَ لَهُمْ: مَا شَأْنُ ابْنِي؟ قَالُوا: جُنَّ بَعْدَكَ ، يَتَكَلَّمُ بِكَلَامٍ مَا نَدْرِي مَا هُوَ؟ فَجَاءَتْ أُمُّهُ ، فَمَسَّتْ جَسَدَهُ ، فَقَالَتْ: لَا ، وَالَّذِي يُحْلَفُ بِهِ ، مَا دَخَلَ جِسْمَ ابْنِي جَانٌّ بَعْدُ، فَسَأَلُوهُ ، فَقَصَّ عَلَيْهِمُ الْقِصَّةَ ، وَالْكَلَامَ الَّذِي تَعَلَّمَ مِنَ الرَّجُلِ، فَقَالَ أَبُوهُ: وَاللَّهِ مَا نَسْمَعُ مِنْكَ إِلَّا حَسَنًا ، فَأَطْلَقُوهُ

Entry77/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 250
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from Abu Burda, from Ka'b, who said:

Matn

“There is no man who says seven times: O God, Sender-down of the Book, Splitter of the clouds, Setter of the scale, O Lord of Majesty and Honor, make me on the Day of Resurrection among the righteous with You; and there is no power and no strength except with God, but he reaches by them the pleasure of God.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ أَبِي بُرْدَةَ، عَنْ كَعْبٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

مَا مِنْ رَجُلٍ يَقُولُ سَبْعَ مَرَّاتٍ: اللَّهُمَّ مُنْزِلَ الْكِتَابِ، مُفْرِجَ السَّحَابِ، وَاضِعَ الْمِيزَانِ، يَا ذَا الْجَلَالِ وَالْإِكْرَامِ ، اجْعَلْنِي يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ عِنْدَكَ مِنَ الْأَبْرَارِ، وَلَا حَوْلَ وَلَا قُوَّةَ إِلَّا بِاللَّهِ ، إِلَّا بَلَغَ بِهِنَّ رِضْوَانَ اللَّهِ»

Entry78/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 251-253
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-Sulb b. Matar al-Khuldi narrated to us, from Qudama, nephew of Sahm b. Minjab, who said: I heard Sahm say:

Matn

We raided Darin with al-'Ala' b. al-Hadrami. He called on God with three supplications and was answered in all of them. He said: We traveled with him and halted at a stopping place; we looked for water for ablution and could not find it. He prayed two rak'as, then supplicated: “O Knowing, O Forbearing, O Most High, O Mighty, we are Your servants, fighting Your enemy in Your path. Give us rain from which we may drink and perform ablution, and when we leave it, let no one but us have a share in it.” He said: We went on, and had not gone far when we came upon a spring of rainwater that gushed forth when the sky cleared. We drank, took provisions, and I filled my water skin, then left it. I said: “I will see whether he is answered.” We went a mile, and I said to my companions: “I forgot my water skin,” and went back to that place; it was as if there had never been any water. I took my skin and rejoined them. When we reached Darin, the sea lay between us and them, and he supplicated: “O God, O Knowing, O Forbearing, O Most High, O Mighty, we are Your servants, in Your path; make for us a way to Your enemy.” Then he plunged us into the sea. By God, our saddles did not get wet until we came out to them, and not one man of us was held back. When we returned, he [al-'Ala'] complained of his belly and died. We found no water to wash him, so we wrapped him in his clothes and buried him. We had not gone far when we came upon abundant water. Some of us said to others: “Return with us so that we may exhume him and wash him.” We returned and looked for his grave, but its place was hidden from us. When we could not find it, a man of the group said: “Return, do not do it, for I heard him supplicate: 'O Knowing, O Forbearing, O Most High, O Mighty, hide my grave, let no one see my body, and let no one see my nakedness.'” So we returned and left him.

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الصُّلْبُ بْنُ مَطَرٍ الْخُلْدِيُّ، عَنْ قُدَامَةَ ابْنِ أُخْتِ سَهْمِ بْنِ مِنْجَابٍ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ سَهْمًا، يَقُولُ:

Matn

غَزَوْنَا مَعَ الْعَلَاءِ بْنِ الْحَضْرَمِيِّ دَارِينَ قَالَ: فَدَعَا اللَّهَ ثَلَاثَ دَعَوَاتٍ ، فَاسْتُجِيبَ لَهُ فِيهِنَّ كُلِّهِنَّ ، قَالَ: سِرْنَا مَعَهُ ، قَالَ: فَنَزَلْنَا مَنْزِلًا ، فَطَلَبْنَا الْوَضُوءَ ، فَلَمْ نَقْدِرْ عَلَيْهِ، فَصَلَّى رَكْعَتَيْنِ، ثُمَّ دَعَا فَقَالَ: «يَا عَلِيمُ، يَا حَلِيمُ، يَا عَلِيُّ، يَا عَظِيمُ، إِنَّا عَبِيدُكَ ، وَفِي سَبِيلِكَ نُقَاتِلُ عَدُوَّكَ، اسْقِنَا غَيْثًا نَشْرَبُ مِنْهَا وَنَتَوَضَّأُ، وَإِذَا تَرَكْنَاهُ فَلَا تَجْعَلْ لِأَحَدٍ فِيهِ نَصِيبًا غَيْرَنَا» . قَالَ: فَسِرْنَا ، فَمَا جَاوَزْنَا غَيْرَ بَعِيدٍ، فَإِذَا نَحْنُ بِعَيْنٍ مِنْ مَاءِ سَمَاءٍ ، حِينَ انْقَلَعَتْ عَنْهُ السَّمَاءُ تَدَفَّقَ، قَالَ: فَشَرِبْنَا، وَتَزَوَّدْنَا، وَمَلَأْتُ إِدَاوَتِي، ثُمَّ تَرَكْنَاهَا ، وَقُلْتُ: لَأَنْظُرَنَّ ، هَلِ اسْتُجِيبَ لَهُ؟ قَالَ: فَسِرْنَا مِيلًا ، وَقُلْتُ لِأَصْحَابِي: نَسِيتُ إِدَاوَتِي، فَذَهَبْتُ إِلَى ذَلِكَ الْمَكَانِ، فَكَأَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَاءٌ قَطُّ ، قَالَ: فَأَخَذْتُ إِدَاوَتِي ، وَجِئْتُهُمْ، فَلَمَّا أَتَيْنَا دَارِينَ وَبَيْنَنَا وَبَيْنَهُمُ الْبَحْرُ، فَدَعَا فَقَالَ: «اللَّهُمَّ يَا عَلِيمُ، يَا حَلِيمُ، يَا عَلِيُّ، يَا عَظِيمُ، إِنَّا عِبَادُكَ ، وَفِي سَبِيلِكَ ، اجْعَلْ لَنَا سَبِيلًا إِلَى عَدُوِّكَ» . ثُمَّ تَقَحَّمَ بِنَا فِي الْبَحْرِ، فَوَاللَّهِ مَا ابْتَلَّتْ سُرُجُنَا حَتَّى خَرَجْنَا إِلَيْهِمْ ، وَمَا احْتَبَسَ مِنَّا رَجُلٌ، فَلَمَّا رَجَعْنَا اشْتَكَى الْبَطْنَ فَمَاتَ، فَلَمْ نَجِدْ مَاءً نُغَسِّلُهُ ، فَلَفَفْنَاهُ فِي ثِيَابِهِ ، وَدَفَنَّاهُ، فَمَا سِرْنَا غَيْرَ بَعِيدٍ ، إِذَا مَاءٌ كَثِيرٌ، فَقَالَ بَعْضُنَا لِبَعْضٍ: ارْجِعُوا بِنَا حَتَّى نَسْتَخْرِجَهُ ، فَنُغَسِّلَهُ، فَرَجَعْنَا فَطَلَبْنَا قَبْرَهُ، فَخَفِيَ عَلَيْنَا مَوْضِعُهُ، فَلَمَّا لَمْ نَقْدِرْ عَلَيْهِ، قَالَ رَجُلٌ مِنَ الْقَوْمِ: ارْجِعُوا ، لَا تَفْعَلُوا ، فَإِنِّي قَدْ سَمِعْتُهُ يَدْعُو اللَّهَ يَقُولُ: «يَا عَلِيمُ، يَا حَلِيمُ، يَا عَلِيُّ، يَا عَظِيمُ ، اخْفِ جَدَثِي، وَلَا تُطْلِعْ أَحَدًا عَلَى جَسَدِي، وَلَا يَرَى أَحَدٌ عَوْرَتِي» . قَالَ: فَرَجَعْنَا وَتَرَكْنَاهُ

Entry79/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 254-256
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Ata' b. al-Sa'ib narrated to us, who said: When the people were routed on the Day of al-Jamajim, Abu al-Bakhtari al-Ta'i began to urge the people on, and I heard him say:

Matn

“There was a prophet of the Children of Israel who appeared, and those whom God willed followed him. He married the daughter of one of the believers who followed him, who was among the best of his companions, and she bore him a boy. When the boy grew up and came of age, he sought out the Christians, and they supported him and tied banners for him. He marched with them against his father, killed his father and his believing grandfather, the father of his mother, and prevailed over them, except for a small band of believers. While he had prevailed over them, it occurred to him that the believers had declared war on them, so he went out with those who were with him, thinking of them as a mouthful of meat, and they fought. God gave the believers victory over them and they routed them, and the prophet's son was taken captive. They crucified him alive, for that is how they did at that time, so that he would die his own death and not be killed. As he was calling on God by his gods and crying out to the gods by their names, calling on them to deliver him from what he was in, he cried out one night, and when he feared the dawn he called on God and said: 'O God, deliver me and save me.' The guards' bonds fell away from him and he left, and they could not find him. That weighed heavily on the believers. Then it was revealed to one of the believers in his sleep that he had called on his gods and they had not answered him, but he called on Me and I answered him, and I am not like the deaf and dumb who do not understand.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَطَاءُ بْنُ السَّائِبِ قَالَ: لَمَّا انْهَزَمَ النَّاسُ يَوْمَ الْجَمَاجِمِ جَعَلَ أَبُو الْبَخْتَرِيِّ الطَّائِيُّ يُحَرِّضُ النَّاسَ ، فَسَمِعْتُهُ يَقُولُ: «

Matn

كَانَ نَبِيٌّ مِنْ بَنِي إِسْرَائِيلَ قَدْ ظَهَرَ ، وَتَبِعَهُ مَنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ، وَإِنَّهُ تَزَوَّجَ بِنْتَ رَجُلٍ مِمَّنْ تَابَعَهُ مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، وَكَانَ مِنْ أَفْضَلِ أَصْحَابِهِ، فَوَلَدَتْ لَهُ غُلَامًا، فَلَمَّا شَبَّ وَبَلَغَ ، تَتَبَّعَ النَّصَارَى فَنَصَّرُوهُ ، وَعَقَدُوا لَهُ أَلْوِيَتَهُمْ، فَخَرَجَ بِهِمْ عَلَى أَبِيهِ، فَقَتَلَ أَبَاهُ ، وَجَدَّهُ الْمُؤْمِنَ أَبَا أُمِّهِ، وَظَهَرَ عَلَيْهِمْ إِلَّا شِرْذِمَةً قَلِيلَةً مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، فَبَيْنَا هُوَ قَدْ ظَهَرَ عَلَيْهِمْ فِي نَفْسِهِ أَنَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ قَدْ آذَنُوا لَكُمْ بِالْحَرْبِ، فَخَرَجَ بِمَنْ مَعَهُ ، وَهُوَ يَرَاهُمْ كَأَكلَةِ رَأْسٍ ، فَاقْتَتَلُوا، فَأَظْهَرَ اللَّهُ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ عَلَيْهِمْ ، فَهَزَمُوهُمْ، فَأُخِذَ ابْنُ النَّبِيِّ أَسِيرًا، فَصَلَبُوهُ وَهُوَ حَيٌّ، وَكَذَلِكَ كَانُوا يَفْعَلُونَ فِي ذَلِكَ الزَّمَانِ حَتَّى يَمُوتَ مَوْتَةَ نَفْسِهِ ، وَلَا يُقْتَلَ، فَبَيْنَا هُوَ يَدْعُو اللَّهَ بِآلِهَتِهِ، وَيَهْتِفُ بِالْآلِهَةِ، وَيَهْتِفُ بِأَسْمَائِهَا يَدْعُوهَا أَنْ تُخَلِّصَهُ مِمَّا هُوَ فِيهِ، فَهَتَفَ لَيْلَةً ، حَتَّى إِذَا خَافَ الصُّبْحَ دَعَا اللَّهَ ، فَقَالَ: يَا اللَّهُ ، خَلِّصْنِي وَنَجِّنِي، فَتَقَطَّعَتْ عَنْهُ الشُّرَطُ فَذَهَبَ ، فَلَمْ يَقْدِرُوا عَلَيْهِ، فَكَبُرَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى الْمُؤْمِنِينَ ، وَاشْتَدَّ عَلَيْهِمْ ، قَالَ: فَأُوحِيَ إِلَى رَجُلٍ مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ فِي مَنَامِهِ ، أَنَّهُ دَعَا آلِهَتَهُ ، فَلَمْ تُجِبْهُ، وَدَعَانِي فَأَجَبْتُهُ، وَلَمْ أَكُنْ كَالصُّمِّ الْبُكْمِ الَّذِينَ لَا يَعْقِلُونَ»

Entry80/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 256-257
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Umar b. Hafs narrated to us, from Ghalib al-Qattan, who said:

Matn

I said to al-Hasan: “O Abu Sa'id, we attend the mosque, and in it are the hypocrite, the drinker of wine, the sodomite and the transgressor; how should we supplicate?” He said: “Include everyone in supplication and counsel to the common people, for you are only an intercessor; if God gives you what you want, [well and good], and if not, you have fulfilled what was upon you.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عُمَرُ بْنُ حَفْصٍ، عَنْ غَالِبٍ الْقَطَّانِ، قَالَ:

Matn

قُلْتُ لِلْحَسَنِ: يَا أَبَا سَعِيدٍ إِنَّا نَحْضُرُ الْمَسْجِدَ ، وَفِيهِ الْمُنَافِقُ ، وَشَارِبُ الْخَمْرِ ، وَاللُّوطِيُّ وَالْفَاسِقُ ، فَكَيْفَ نَدْعُو؟ قَالَ: «تَعُمُّ فِي الدُّعَاءِ وَالنُّصْحِ لِلْعَامَّةِ، فَإِنَّمَا أَنْتَ شَافِعٌ، فَإِنْ يُعْطِكَ اللَّهُ الَّذِي تُرِيدُ، وَإِلَّا فَقَدْ قَضَيْتَ الَّذِي عَلَيْكَ»

Entry81/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 257
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Abd al-Rahman b. Ishaq narrated to us, from al-Qasim b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from 'Abd Allah b. Mas'ud, who said:

Matn

“Supplications for relief: O God, there is no god but You; I ask You for bounty and mercy, You are their Guardian, none but You is their Guardian. My Lord, I have wronged myself, so grant me well-being.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

دَعَوَاتُ الْفَرَجِ: اللَّهُمَّ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا أَنْتَ ، أَسْأَلُكَ الْفَضْلَ وَالرَّحْمَةَ، أَنْتَ وَلِيُّهُمَا ، لَا يَلِيهِمَا غَيْرُكَ، رَبِّ ظَلَمْتُ نَفْسِي فَعَافِنِي»

Transmission overview

Entry82/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 257-258
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Ata' b. al-Sa'ib narrated to us, from his father, who said: I was with 'Ammar b. Yasir, and he used to make a supplication in his prayer. A man came to him and said: “O Abu al-Yaqzan, teach me this supplication.” He said: “If you want it, come to me at my family's home.” He came to him there, and 'Ammar said to him:

Matn

Say: “O God, by Your knowledge of the unseen and Your power over creation, keep me alive as long as You know life to be better for me, and take me when You know death to be better for me. O God, I ask You for fear of You in secret and in public, the word of truth in contentment and in anger, moderation in wealth and in poverty. I ask You for contentment after the decree, the coolness of life after death, the pleasure of looking upon Your face and longing for meeting You, without harmful hardship or misleading trial. O God, adorn us with the adornment of faith and make us guides who are guided.” Then he said: “Shall I not teach you some words” (as if he were raising them to the Prophet) “better than these? When you lay your side [to sleep], say: O God, I have submitted myself to You, turned my face to You, set my back against You, and entrusted my affair to You; I believe in Your Book that You sent down and Your Prophet whom You sent. O God, You created my soul and to You belong its life and its death: if You take it, have mercy on it, and if You delay it, protect it with the protection of faith.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَطَاءُ بْنُ السَّائِبِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، قَالَ: كُنْتُ عِنْدَ عَمَّارِ بْنِ يَاسِرٍ فَكَانَ يَدْعُو بِدُعَاءٍ فِي صَلَاتِهِ، فَأَتَاهُ رَجُلٌ فَقَالَ: يَا أَبَا الْيَقْظَانِ، عَلِّمْنِي هَذَا الدُّعَاءَ، فَقَالَ: إِنْ أَنْتَ أَرَدْتَهُ، فَأْتِنِي فِي أَهْلِي. قَالَ: فَأَتَاهُ فِي أَهْلِهِ، فَقَالَ لَهُ عَمَّارٌ

Matn

قُلِ: «اللَّهُمَّ بِعِلْمِكَ الْغَيْبَ، وَقُدْرَتِكَ عَلَى الْخَلْقِ، أَحْيِنِي مَا عَلِمْتَ الْحَيَاةَ خَيْرًا لِي، وَاقْبِضْنِي إِذَا عَلِمْتَ أَنَّ الْوَفَاةَ خَيْرٌ لِي، اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ الْخَشْيَةَ فِي الْغَيْبِ وَالشَّهَادَةِ، وَكَلِمَةَ الْحَقِّ فِي الرِّضَا وَالْغَضَبِ، وَالْقَصْدَ فِي الْغِنَى وَالْفَقْرِ، وَأَسْأَلُكَ الرِّضَا بَعْدَ الْقَضَاءِ، وَبَرَدَ الْعَيْشِ بَعْدَ الْمَوْتِ، وَأَسْأَلُكَ لَذَّةَ النَّظَرِ فِي وَجْهِكَ، وَشَوْقًا إِلَى لِقَائِكَ، مِنْ غَيْرِ ضَرَّاءَ مُضِرَّةٍ أَوْ فِتْنَةٍ مُضِلَّةٍ، اللَّهُمَّ زَيِّنَّا بِزِينَةِ الْإِيمَانِ، وَاجْعَلْنَا مِنَ الْهُدَاةِ الْمُهْتَدِينَ» . ثُمَّ قَالَ: «أَلَا أُعَلِّمُكَ كَلِمَاتٍ ، - كَأَنَّهُ يَرْفَعُهُنَّ - هُوَ أَحْسَنُ مِنْهُنَّ، إِذَا وَضَعْتَ جَنْبَكَ فَقُلِ: اللَّهُمَّ أَسْلَمْتُ نَفْسِي إِلَيْكَ، وَوَجَّهْتُ وَجْهِي إِلَيْكَ، وَأَلْجَأْتُ ظَهْرِي إِلَيْكَ، وَفَوَّضْتُ أَمْرِي إِلَيْكَ، آمَنْتُ بِكِتَابِكَ الْمُنَزَّلِ، وَنَبِيِّكَ الْمُرْسَلِ، اللَّهُمَّ نَفْسِي خَلَقْتَهَا ، لَكَ مَحْيَاهَا ، وَلَكَ مَمَاتُهَا، فَإِنْ قَبَضْتَهَا فَارْحَمْهَا، وَإِنْ أَخَّرْتَهَا فَاحْفَظْهَا ، بِحِفْظِ الْإِيمَانِ»

  2. 02Father of 'Ata' b. al-Sa'ibابيه

Entry83/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 259
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Umara b. al-Qa'qa' narrated to us, from Abu Zur'a, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Two words, light on the tongue, heavy in the balance, beloved to the Merciful: Glory be to God and with His praise; Glory be to God the Almighty.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عُمَارَةُ بْنُ الْقَعْقَاعِ، عَنْ أَبِي زُرْعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

كَلِمَتَانِ خَفِيفَتَانِ عَلَى اللِّسَانِ ، ثَقِيلَتَانِ فِي الْمِيزَانِ ، حَبِيبَتَانِ إِلَى الرَّحْمَنِ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ ، سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ الْعَظِيمِ»

Entry84/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 260
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Husayn narrated to us, from Mujahid, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr, who said:

Matn

“No assembly has ever gathered remembering God but God remembers them in an assembly more honored and noble; and no people have dispersed having not remembered God in their gathering but it will be a regret to them on the Day of Resurrection.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حُصَيْنٌ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو قَالَ: «

Matn

مَا اجْتَمَعَ مَلَأٌ قَطُّ يَذْكُرُونَ اللَّهَ ، إِلَّا ذَكَرَهُمُ اللَّهُ فِي مَلَإٍ أَعَزَّ وَأَكْرَمَ، وَمَا تَفَرَّقَ قَوْمٌ لَمْ يَذْكُرُوا اللَّهَ فِي مَجْلِسِهِمْ ، إِلَّا كَانَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَسْرَةً يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ»

  2. 02Mujahidمجاهد
  3. 03Husaynحصين

Entry85/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 262
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Asim al-Ahwal narrated to us, from Abu 'Uthman, from Salman al-Farisi, who said:

Matn

“When a man remembers God in ease and praises Him in prosperity, then harm strikes him and he calls on God, the angels say: A familiar voice, from a weak person. And they ask forgiveness for him. And when he does not remember God in ease and does not praise Him in prosperity, then harm strikes him and he calls on God, the angels say: An unfamiliar voice.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَاصِمٌ الْأَحْوَلُ، عَنْ أَبِي عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ سَلْمَانَ الْفَارِسِيِّ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِذَا كَانَ الرَّجُلُ يَذْكُرُ اللَّهَ فِي السَّرَّاءِ وَيَحْمَدُهُ فِي الرَّخَاءِ ، فَأَصَابَهُ ضُرٌّ فَدَعَا اللَّهَ، قَالَتِ الْمَلَائِكَةُ: صَوْتٌ مَعْرُوفٌ ، مِنِ امْرِئٍ ضَعِيفٍ. قَالَ: فَتَسْتَغْفِرُ لَهُ. قَالَ: وَإِذَا كَانَ لَا يَذْكُرُ اللَّهَ فِي السَّرَّاءِ ، وَلَا يَحْمَدُهُ فِي الرَّخَاءِ ، فَأَصَابَهُ ضُرٌّ ، فَدَعَا اللَّهَ، قَالَتِ الْمَلَائِكَةُ: صَوْتٌ مُنْكَرٌ»

Transmission path

  1. 01Salman al-FarisiEarliest transmitterسلمان الفارسي
  2. 02Abu 'Uthmanابي عثمان
  3. 03'Asim al-Ahwalعاصم الاحول
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry86/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 262-263
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Mis'ar narrated to us, from Abu Bakr b. Hafs, from al-Hasan b. al-Hasan, who said:

Matn

'Abd Allah b. Ja'far gave his daughter in marriage and was alone with her. Al-Hasan said: I met her and said: “What did he say to you?” She said: “He said to me: 'My daughter, when death comes to you, or something that terrifies you, say: There is no god but God, the Forbearing, the Generous; glory be to God, Lord of the Mighty Throne; praise be to God, Lord of the worlds.'” Then I came to al-Hajjaj and said them, and he said: “You came to me when I wanted to strike off your head, and there is no one of your family now dearer to me than you; ask what you will.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مِسْعَرٌ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَفْصٍ، عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ الْحَسَنِ، قَالَ:

Matn

زَوَّجَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ ابْنَتَهُ ، فَخَلَا بِهَا. قَالَ الْحَسَنُ: فَلَقِيتُهَا ، فَقُلْتُ: مَا قَالَ لَكِ؟ قَالَتْ: قَالَ لِي: «يَا بُنَيَّةُ إِذَا نَزَلَ بِكِ الْمَوْتُ ، أَوْ أَمْرٌ تَفْظَعِينَ بِهِ ، فَقُولِي: لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ الْحَلِيمُ الْكَرِيمُ، سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ رَبِّ الْعَرْشِ الْعَظِيمِ، الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ رَبِّ الْعَالَمِينَ» فَأَتَيْتُ الْحَجَّاجَ ، فَقُلْتُهُنَّ، فَقَالَ: لَقَدْ جِئْتَنِي ، وَأَنَا أُرِيدُ أَنْ أَضْرِبَ عُنُقَكَ، وَمَا مِنْ أَهْلِكَ الْآنَ أَحَدٌ أَحَبَّ إِلَيَّ مِنْكَ فَسَلْ مَا شِئْتَ

  2. 02Abu Bakr b. Hafsأبو بكر بن حفص

Entry87/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 264-265
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from 'Abd al-Malik b. Maysara, who said:

Matn

'Abd Allah b. Mas'ud and 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr met. Ibn Mas'ud said: “For me to say, when I go out until I reach my need, 'Glory be to God, praise be to God, there is no god but God, and God is greatest' is dearer to me than to mount on the like number of horses in the path of God.” And 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr said: “For me to say them is dearer to me than to spend the like number of dinars in the path of God.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ، قَالَ:

Matn

اجْتَمَعَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْعُودٍ وَعَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَمْرٍو فَقَالَ ابْنُ مَسْعُودٍ: «لَأَنْ أَقُولَ إِذَا خَرَجْتُ حَتَّى أَبْلُغَ حَاجَتِي: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ ، أَحَبُّ إِلَيَّ مِنْ أَنْ أَحْمِلَ عَلَى عِدَّتِهِنَّ مِنَ الْجِيَادِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ» وَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَمْرٍو: «لَأَنْ أَقُولَهُنَّ ، أَحَبُّ إِلَيَّ مِنْ أَنْ أُنْفِقَ عِدَّتَهُنَّ دَنَانِيرَ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ»

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd al-Malik b. MaysaraEarliest transmitterعبد الملك بن ميسره
  2. 02al-A'mashالاعمش

Entry88/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 265
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from 'Amr b. Murra, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Shibr, who said:

Matn

I asked Muhammad b. al-Hanafiyya: “Which words are dearest to you on the evening of 'Arafa?” He said: “There is no god but God, and God is greatest.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ مُرَّةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ شَتَرٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

سَأَلْتُ مُحَمَّدَ ابْنَ الْحَنَفِيَّةِ: أَيُّ الْكَلَامِ أَحَبُّ إِلَيْكَ عَشِيَّةَ عَرَفَةَ؟ قَالَ: «لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd al-Rahman b. ShibrEarliest transmitterعبد الرحمن بن شتر

Entry89/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 266
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from Abu Salih, from one of the Companions of the Prophet ﷺ, who said: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“The words dearest to God are four; it does not harm you with which of them you begin: Glory be to God, praise be to God, there is no god but God, and God is greatest.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ بَعْضِ أَصْحَابِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ، قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

إِنَّ أَحَبَّ الْكَلَامِ إِلَى اللَّهِ أَرْبَعٌ ، لَا يَضُرُّكَ بِأَيِّهِنَّ بَدَأْتَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ»

Entry91/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 268
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Dirar b. Murra narrated to us, from a man of the Banu 'Abs, from Abu al-Darda', who said:

Matn

It reached him that a man had freed a hundred slaves, and he said: “A hundred slaves out of one man's wealth is a great deal, but better than that and better still is a faith that is constant by night and day, and that the tongue of one of you remain moist with the remembrance of God.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا ضِرَارُ بْنُ مُرَّةَ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ مِنْ بَنِي عَبْسٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الدَّرْدَاءِ قَالَ:

Matn

بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلًا أَعْتَقَ مِائَةَ نَسَمَةٍ ، فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ مِائَةَ نَسَمَةٍ مِنْ مَالِ رَجُلٍ لَكَثِيرٌ، وَأَفْضَلُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَأَفْضَلُ: إِيمَانٌ مَلْزُومٌ بِاللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ، وَلَا يَزَالُ لِسَانُ أَحَدِكُمْ رَطْبًا مِنْ ذِكْرِ اللَّهِ»

Transmission path

Entry92/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 269
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Fitr narrated to us, from Mundhir al-Thawri, who said: I heard Muhammad b. al-Hanafiyya say:

Matn

“What will one of you do with speech beyond seven [things]: glory be to God, praise be to God, there is no god but God, God is greatest, enjoining the good, forbidding the wrong, asking for good, and seeking refuge from evil?”

العربية
Isnad

Matn

مَا يَصْنَعُ أَحَدُكُمْ بِالْكَلَامِ بَعْدَ سَبْعٍ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، وَأَمْرٍ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ، وَنَهْيٍ عَنِ الْمُنْكَرِ، وَمَسْأَلَةٍ الْخَيْرِ، وَتَعَوُّذٍ مِنَ الشَّرِّ؟»

Entry93/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 269-270
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Fitr narrated to us, from al-Muthanna b. al-Sabbah, from 'Ata' al-Khurasani, who said: I heard Ibn 'Umar say: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Whoever says 'Glory be to God, praise be to God, there is no god but God, and God is greatest,' God writes for him ten good deeds for each one of them and raises him ten degrees by them, and whoever adds, God adds to him. Whoever's intercession stands in the way of one of the limits of God has opposed God in His command; whoever contends in a false dispute, or helps in a false dispute, remains in the wrath of God until he is finished; and whoever slanders a believing man or woman without knowledge, God will hold him on the Day of Resurrection in the mire of ruin until he comes out from what he said, and he will not come out.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا فِطْرٌ، عَنِ الْمُثَنَّى بْنِ الصَّبَّاحِ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ الْخُرَاسَانِيِّ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ ابْنَ عُمَرَ، يَقُولُ: قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

مَنْ قَالَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ ، كَتَبَ اللَّهُ لَهُ بِكُلِّ وَاحِدَةٍ مِنْهُنَّ عَشْرَ حَسَنَاتٍ، وَرَفَعَ لَهُ بِهِنَّ عَشْرَ دَرَجَاتٍ، وَمَنْ زَادَ زَادَهُ اللَّهُ، وَمَنْ حَالَتْ شَفَاعَتُهُ دُونَ حَدٍّ مِنْ حُدُودِ اللَّهِ ضَادَّ اللَّهَ فِي أَمْرِهِ، وَمَنْ خَاصَمَ خُصُومَةَ بَاطِلٍ ، وَأَعَانَ عَلَى خُصُومَةِ بَاطِلٍ ، كَانَ فِي سَخَطِ اللَّهِ حَتَّى يَفْرُغَ، وَمَنْ يَغْتَبْ مُؤْمِنًا ، أَوْ مُؤْمِنَةً بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ ، حَبَسَهُ اللَّهُ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ فِي رَدْغَةِ الْخَبَالِ حَتَّى يَخْرُجَ مِمَّا قَالَ ، وَلَيْسَ بِخَارِجٍ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Ibn 'UmarEarliest transmitterابن عمر
  2. 02'Ata' al-Khurasaniعطاء الخراساني
  3. 03al-Muthanna b. al-Sabbahالمثنى بن الصباح
  4. 04Fitrفطر
  5. 05Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Isnad

“When the servant says 'Glory be to God,' the angel says 'and with His praise'; if he says 'Glory be to God and with His praise,' the angels pray for him.”

Matn

Entry95/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 273
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Mis'ar narrated to us, from 'Atiyya, from Abu Sa'id, who said:

Matn

“When the servant says 'Praise be to God, abundantly,' the angel says: How shall I write it? He says: Write for him My mercy abundantly. And when he says 'God is greatest, greatly,' the angel says: How shall I write it? He says: Write My mercy abundantly.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مِسْعَرٌ، عَنْ عَطِيَّةَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِذَا قَالَ الْعَبْدُ: الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ كَثِيرًا ، قَالَ الْمَلَكُ: كَيْفَ أَكْتُبُ؟ قَالَ: اكْتُبْ لَهُ رَحْمَتِي كَثِيرًا، وَإِذَا قَالَ: اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ كَبِيرًا. قَالَ الْمَلَكُ: كَيْفَ أَكْتُبُ؟ قَالَ: اكْتُبْ رَحْمَتِي كَثِيرًا»

Entry97/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 274
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from al-Hakam, from 'Ubayd b. 'Umayr al-Laythi, who said:

Matn

“God has apportioned your character among you as He has apportioned your provisions. God gives this world to those He loves and those He does not love, but He gives faith only to those He loves. So whoever among you is too cowardly to fight the enemy, too weak for the night prayer, or too stingy to spend his wealth, let him increase his tasbih, tahmid, takbir and tahlil.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنِ الْحَكَمِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ بْنِ عُمَيْرٍ اللَّيْثِيِّ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ اللَّهَ قَسَمَ بَيْنَكُمْ أَخْلَاقَكُمْ ، كَمَا قَسَمَ بَيْنَكُمْ أَرْزَاقَكُمْ، وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ يُعْطِي الدُّنْيَا مَنْ يُحِبُّ ، وَمَنْ لَا يُحِبُّ ، وَلَا يُعْطِي الْإِيمَانَ إِلَّا مَنْ يُحِبُّ، فَمَنْ جَبُنَ مِنْكُمْ عَنِ الْعَدُوِّ أَنْ يُجَاهِدَهُ، وَعَجَزَ عَنِ اللَّيْلِ أَنْ يُكَابِدَهُ، وَضَنَّ بِمَالِهِ أَنْ يُنْفِقَهُ ، فَلْيُكْثِرْ مِنَ التَّسْبِيحِ وَالتَّحْمِيدِ وَالتَّكْبِيرِ وَالتَّهْلِيلِ»

Entry98/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 277
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Mutarrif narrated to us, from 'Atiyya, from Ibn 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

His words {So remember Me; I will remember you} [al-Baqara 2:152]: “God's remembrance of you is greater than your remembrance of Him.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُطَرِّفٌ، عَنْ عَطِيَّةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ فِي قَوْلِهِ: {وَلَذِكْرُ اللَّهِ أَكْبَرُ} [العنكبوت: 45] قَالَ:

Matn

قَوْلُهُ: {فَاذْكُرُونِي أَذْكُرْكُمْ} [البقرة: 152] «فَذِكْرُ اللَّهِ إِيَّاكُمْ أَكْبَرُ مِنْ ذِكْرِكُمْ إِيَّاهُ»

  2. 02'Atiyyaعطية

  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry99/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 277-278
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Mis'ar narrated to us, from Abu Mus'ab, from Ka'b:

Matn

Musa said: “O Lord, among the states we are in are states in which we do not honor You by remembering You, when one of us is in a state of ritual impurity or is relieving himself.” He said: “O Musa, remember Me in every state.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مِسْعَرٌ، عَنْ أَبِي مُصْعَبٍ، عَنْ كَعْبٍ،

Matn

قَالَ مُوسَى: «يَا رَبِّ، إِنَّ مِنْ حَالِ الَّذِي نَكُونُ عَلَيْهِ مَا نُجِلُّكَ نَذْكُرُكَ عَلَيْهِ تُصِيبُ أَحَدَنَا الْجَنَابَةُ، وَالْغَائِطُ» . قَالَ: «يَا مُوسَى اذْكُرْنِي عَلَى كُلِّ حَالٍ»

Entry100/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 278-279
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Wiqa' al-Asadi narrated to us, from Sa'id b. Jubayr, who said:

Matn

'Umar b. al-Khattab saw a person counting tasbih with [beads or pebbles] he carried, and 'Umar said: “It suffices him of that to say: Glory be to God, filling the heavens and filling the earth, and filling whatever else He wills; praise be to God, filling the heavens and filling the earth, and filling whatever else He wills; and God is greatest, greatly, filling the heavens and filling the earth, and filling whatever else He wills.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا وِقَاءٌ الْأَسَدِيُّ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

رَأَى عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ إِنْسَانًا يُسَبِّحُ بِتَسْبِيحٍ مَعَهُ ، قَالَ عُمَرُ: «إِنَّمَا يُجْزِيهِ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يَقُولَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ مِلْءَ السَّمَوَاتِ وَمِلْءَ الْأَرْضِ، وَمِلْءَ مَا شَاءَ مِنْ شَيْءٍ بَعْدُ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ مِلْءَ السَّمَوَاتِ ، وَمِلْءَ الْأَرْضِ، وَمِلْءَ مَا شَاءَ مِنْ شَيْءٍ بَعْدُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ كَبِيرًا مِلْءَ السَّمَوَاتِ ، وَمِلْءَ الْأَرْضِ، وَمِلْءَ مَا شَاءَ مِنْ شَيْءٍ بَعْدُ»

Entry101/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 279
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Harun b. 'Antara b. Abi Waki' narrated to us, from his father:

Matn

[He] asked Ibn 'Abbas: “Which deed is best?” He said: “The remembrance of God is greater,” until he had repeated it to him three times. Then he said: “No people sit in one of the houses of God studying the Book of God and passing it between them but they are the guests of God, and the angels shade them with their wings, and they are the visitors of God, until they plunge into some other talk. Whoever follows a path seeking knowledge, God makes easy for him by it a path to Paradise; and whoever is slowed by his deeds, his lineage will not hasten him.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا هَارُونُ بْنُ عَنْتَرَةَ بْنِ أَبِي وَكِيعٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

سَأَلَ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ: أَيُّ الْعَمَلِ أَفْضَلُ؟ قَالَ: «ذِكْرُ اللَّهِ أَكْبَرُ» - حَتَّى أَعَادَهَا عَلَيْهِ ثَلَاثَ مَرَّاتٍ - ثُمَّ قَالَ: «مَا جَلَسَ قَوْمٌ فِي بَيْتٍ مِنْ بُيُوتِ اللَّهِ يَدْرُسُونَ كِتَابَ اللَّهِ ، وَيَتَعَاطَوْنَهُ بَيْنَهُمْ ، إِلَّا كَانُوا أَضْيَافًا لِلَّهِ، وَأَطَلَّتْ عَلَيْهِمُ الْمَلَائِكَةُ بِأَجْنِحَتِهَا، وَكَانُوا زُوَّارًا لِلَّهِ ، حَتَّى يَخُوضُوا فِي حَدِيثٍ غَيْرِهِ، وَمَنْ سَلَكَ طَرِيقًا يَطْلُبُ فِيهِ عِلْمًا ، سَهَّلَ اللَّهُ لَهُ بِهِ طَرِيقًا إِلَى الْجَنَّةِ، وَمَنْ يُبْطِئْ بِهِ عَمَلُهُ ، لَا يُسْرِعْ بِهِ نَسَبُهُ»

Entry102/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 280-281
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Thirwan, from Hudhayl, from Ibn Mas'ud, who said:

Matn

“When God brought Musa near in intimate converse at Tur Sinai, he saw a servant sitting in the shade of the Throne and asked: 'My Lord, who is this?' He did not name him or give his lineage, but said: 'This is a servant who does not envy people for what God has given them of His bounty, is dutiful to his parents, and does not go about with slander.' He said: 'What have you come seeking, O Musa?' He said: 'I came seeking guidance.' He said: 'You have found it, O Musa.' He said: 'O God, forgive me what has passed of my sin and what remains, and what You know better than I; O God, I seek refuge in You from the whisperings of my soul and from the evil of my deeds.' He said: 'You are sufficed, O Musa.' He said: 'My Lord, which deeds are dearest to You for me to do?' He said: 'That you call on Me and do not forget Me.' He said: 'My Lord, which of Your servants is best in deed, that I may do the like of his deed?' He said: 'He whose tongue does not lie, whose heart does not sin, and whose private part does not fornicate.' He said: 'My Lord, who can bear not to be tempted and to lie?' He said: 'My Lord, which of Your servants after that is best in deed?' He said: 'A believer of good character.' He said: 'My Lord, which of Your servants after that is worst in deed?' He said: 'A wicked heart with bad character.' He said: 'Which of Your servants is worst in deed?' He said: 'The carcass of the night, idle by day.'”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ ثَرْوَانَ، عَنْ هُزَيْلٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ مُوسَى لَمَّا قَرَّبَهُ اللَّهُ نَجِيًّا بِطُورِ سَيْنَاءَ، أَبْصَرَ عَبْدًا جَالِسًا فِي ظِلِّ الْعَرْشِ ، سَأَلَهُ: أَيْ رَبِّ ، مَنْ هَذَا؟ فَلَمْ يَنْسِبْهُ ، أَوْ يُسَمِّهِ. قَالَ: هَذَا عَبْدٌ ، لَا يَحْسُدُ النَّاسَ عَلَى مَا آتَاهُمُ اللَّهُ مِنْ فَضْلِهِ، بَرٌّ بِالْوَالِدَيْنِ ، لَا يَمْشِي بِالنَّمِيمَةِ قَالَ: إِيشْ جِئْتَ تَبْتَغِي يَا مُوسَى؟ قَالَ: جِئْتُ أَبْتَغِي الْهُدَى. قَالَ: فَقَدْ وَجَدْتَهُ يَا مُوسَى. قَالَ: اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي مَا خَلَا مِنْ ذَنْبِي ، وَمَا غَبَرَ، وَمَا أَنْتَ أَعْلَمُ بِهِ مِنِّي، اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ وَسْوَسَةِ نَفْسِي، وَمِنْ شَرِّ عَمَلِي. قَالَ: كُفِيتَ يَا مُوسَى. قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ الْأَعْمَالِ أَحَبُّ إِلَيْكَ أَنْ أَعْمَلَ؟ قَالَ: تَدْعُونِي ، فَلَا تَنْسَانِي قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ الْعِبَادِ خَيْرٌ عَمَلًا أَنْ أَعْمَلَ بِمِثْلِ عَمَلِهِ؟ قَالَ: مَنْ لَا يَكْذِبُ لِسَانُهُ، وَلَا يَفْجُرُ قَلْبُهُ، وَلَا يَزْنِي فَرْجُهُ قَالَ: رَبِّ ، وَمَنْ يُطِيقُ أَنْ لَا يَفْتَتِنَ وَيَكْذِبَ؟ قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ عَلَى أَثَرِ ذَلِكَ أَحْسَنُ عَمَلًا؟ قَالَ: مُؤْمِنٌ فِي خُلُقٍ حَسَنٍ. قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ عَلَى أَثَرِ ذَلِكَ شَرٌّ عَمَلًا؟ قَالَ: قَلْبٌ فَاجِرٌ ، فِي خُلُقٍ سَيِّئٍ قَالَ: أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ أَشَرُّ عَمَلًا؟ قَالَ: جِيفَةُ اللَّيْلِ، بَطَّالُ النَّهَارِ»

  2. 02Hudhaylهزيل
  3. 03'Abd al-Rahman b. Thirwanعبد الرحمن بن ثروان
  4. 04Laythليث

Entry103/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 282
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-'Ala' b. al-Musayyab narrated to us, from Abu Ishaq, from Maytham, who said:

Matn

“When God brought Musa near in intimate converse at Tur Sinai, he said: 'Which of Your servants is dearest to You?' He said: 'The one who remembers Me most.' He said: 'Which of Your servants is greatest?' He said: 'A scholar who seeks knowledge.' He said: 'My Lord, which of Your servants is most patient?' He said: 'The one who swallows anger the most.' He said: 'My Lord, which of Your servants is most forbearing?' He said: 'The one who has most control of himself in anger.'”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْعَلَاءُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ مِيثَمٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

لَمَّا قَرَّبَ اللَّهُ مُوسَى نَجِيًّا بِطُورِ سَيْنَاءَ ، قَالَ: أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ أَحَبُّ إِلَيْكَ؟ قَالَ: أَكْثَرُهُمْ ذِكْرًا. قَالَ: أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ أَعْظَمُ؟ قَالَ: عَالِمٌ يَلْتَمِسُ الْعِلْمَ. قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ أَصْبَرُ؟ قَالَ: أَكْظَمُهُمْ عَلَى الْغَيْظِ. قَالَ: رَبِّ ، أَيُّ عِبَادِكَ أَحْلَمُ؟ قَالَ: أَمْلَكُهُمْ لِنَفْسِهِ عِنْدَ الْغَضَبِ»

  3. 03al-'Ala' b. al-Musayyabالعلاء بن المسيب

Entry104/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 282-284
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Umar b. Dharr narrated to us, from his father, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ went up to a group of his Companions, among them 'Abd Allah b. Rawaha, who was reminding them of God. When he saw the Messenger of God ﷺ he fell silent, and the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Remind your companions.” He said: “O Messenger of God, you are more entitled than I.” He said: “You are those with whom God commanded me to keep myself patient,” and he recited to them {And keep yourself patient with those who call upon their Lord} [al-Kahf 18:28] to the end of the verse. He said: “Never does a group of your number sit remembering God but an equal number of angels sit with them: if they praise God, they praise Him; if they glorify God, they glorify Him; if they magnify God, they magnify Him; if they seek God's forgiveness, they say amen. Then they ascend to their Lord, and He asks them (and He knows better than they): 'Where? and from where?' They say: 'Our Lord, servants of Yours from the people of the earth, who remembered You and we remembered You.' He says: 'And what do they say?' They say: 'Our Lord, they praised You.' He says: 'I am the first to be worshipped and the last to be praised.' They say: 'And they glorified You.' He says: 'My praise is not fitting for anyone but Me.' They say: 'Our Lord, they magnified You.' He says: 'Mine is the majesty in the heavens and the earth, and I am the Mighty, the Wise.' They say: 'Our Lord, they sought Your forgiveness.' He says: 'I make you witnesses that I have forgiven them.' They say: 'Our Lord, among them is so-and-so and so-and-so.' He says: 'They are the people whose companion is not made wretched through them.'” 'Umar said: I mentioned that to Mujahid, and he agreed with my father in this hadith, raising it to the Prophet ﷺ, with the like of it.

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عُمَرُ بْنُ ذَرٍّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ

Matn

رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ دَفَعَ إِلَى نَفَرٍ مِنْ أَصْحَابِهِ فِيهِمْ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ رَوَاحَةَ يُذَكِّرُهُمْ بِاللَّهِ، فَلَمَّا رَأَى رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ سَكَتَ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «ذَكِّرْ أَصْحَابَكَ» . فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَنْتَ أَحَقُّ مِنِّي. قَالَ: «أَمَا إِنَّكُمُ الَّذِينَ أَمَرَنِي اللَّهُ أَنْ أَصْبِرَ نَفْسِيَ مَعَهُمْ» ، ثُمَّ تَلَا عَلَيْهِمْ: {وَاصْبِرْ نَفْسَكَ مَعَ الَّذِينَ يَدْعُونَ رَبَّهُمْ} [الكهف: 28] الْآيَةَ ، إِلَى آخِرِهَا ، قَالَ: «وَمَا قَعَدَ عِدَّتُكُمْ قَطُّ يَذْكُرُونَ اللَّهَ ، إِلَّا قَعَدَ مَعَهُمْ عَدَدُهُمْ مِنَ الْمَلَائِكَةِ، فَإِنْ حَمِدُوا اللَّهَ حَمَدُوهُ، وَإِنْ سَبَّحُوا اللَّهَ سَبَّحُوهُ، وَإِنْ كَبَّرُوا اللَّهَ كَبَّرُوهُ، وَإِنِ اسْتَغْفَرُوا اللَّهَ أَمَّنُوا، ثُمَّ عَرَجُوا إِلَى رَبِّهِمْ، فَسَأَلَهُمْ - وَهُوَ أَعْلَمُ مِنْهُمْ - فَقَالَ: أَيْنَ؟ وَمِنْ أَيْنَ؟ قَالُوا: رَبَّنَا عَبِيدٌ لَكَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْأَرْضِ ، ذَكَرُوكَ فَذَكَرْنَاكَ، قَالَ: وَيَقُولُونَ مَاذَا ؟ قَالُوا: رَبَّنَا حَمَدُوكَ. قَالَ: أَنَا أَوَّلُ مَنْ عُبِدَ ، وَآخِرُ مَنْ حُمِدَ. قَالُوا: وَسَبَّحُوكَ. قَالَ: مَدْحِي لَا يَنْبَغِي لِأَحَدٍ غَيْرِي. قَالُوا: رَبَّنَا كَبَّرُوكَ. قَالَ: لِيَ الْكِبْرِيَاءُ فِي السَّمَوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ ، وَأَنَا الْعَزِيزُ الْحَكِيمُ. قَالُوا: رَبَّنَا اسْتَغْفَرُوكَ. قَالَ: إِنِّي أُشْهِدُكُمْ أَنِّي قَدْ غَفَرْتُ لَهُمْ. قَالُوا: رَبَّنَا فِيهِمْ فُلَانٌ وَفُلَانٌ، قَالَ: هُمُ الْقَوْمُ لَا يَشْقَى بِهِمْ جَلِيسُهُمْ» قَالَ عُمَرُ: فَذَكَرْتُ ذَلِكَ لِمُجَاهِدٍ، فَوَافَقَ أَبِي فِي هَذَا الْحَدِيثِ، يَرْفَعُهُ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ ﷺ ، مِثْلَهُ

  2. 02'Umar b. Dharrعمر بن ذر
  3. 03Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry105/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 284
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Isma'il b. Abi Khalid narrated to us, from 'Awn b. 'Abd Allah, from 'Abd Allah b. Mas'ud, who said:

Matn

“Whoever says 'There is no god but God, praise be to God, God is greatest, and glory be to God,' an angel ascends with it to heaven and does not pass any assembly of angels but they ask forgiveness for its speaker, until it is presented before the Face of the Lord of the worlds.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ بْنُ أَبِي خَالِدٍ، عَنْ عَوْنِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

مَنْ قَالَ: لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، وَسُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، عَرَجَ بِهَا مَلَكٌ إِلَى السَّمَاءِ ، فَلَا يَمُرُّ عَلَى مَلَإٍ مِنَ الْمَلَائِكَةِ إِلَّا اسْتَغْفَرُوا لِصَاحِبِهَا ، حَتَّى يُحَيَّى بِهَا وَجْهُ رَبِّ الْعَالَمِينَ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. Mas'udEarliest transmitterعبد الله بن مسعود
  2. 02'Awn b. 'Abd Allahعون بن عبد الله
  3. 03Isma'il b. Abi Khalidإسماعيل بن أبي خالد
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry106/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 285
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from Ibrahim al-Taymi, from al-Harith b. Suwayd, who said: 'Abd Allah [b. Mas'ud] said:

Matn

“Among the words dearest to God is for a man to say: Glory be to You, O God, and with Your praise; blessed is Your name, exalted is Your majesty, and there is no god other than You.” He said: “I have wronged myself, so forgive me, for none forgives sins but You. And among the greatest of sins with God is for a man to say to another: 'Fear God,' and he answers: 'Mind yourself.'”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ سُوَيْدٍ، قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ: «

Matn

إِنَّ مِنْ أَحَبِّ الْكَلَامِ إِلَى اللَّهِ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ: سُبْحَانَكَ اللَّهُمَّ وَبِحَمْدِكَ، وَتَبَارَكَ اسْمُكَ، وَتَعَالَى جَدُّكَ، وَلَا إِلَهَ غَيْرُكَ» . قَالَ: «إِنِّي قَدْ ظَلَمْتُ نَفْسِي ، فَاغْفِرْ لِي، إِنَّهُ لَا يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلَّا أَنْتَ، وَإِنَّ مِنْ أَكْبَرِ الذُّنُوبِ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ: اتَّقِ اللَّهَ ، فَيَقُولَ: عَلَيْكَ نَفْسَكَ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. Mas'udEarliest transmitterعبد الله بن مسعود
  2. 02al-Harith b. Suwaydالحارث بن سويد
  3. 03Ibrahim al-Taymiإبراهيم التيمي
  4. 04al-A'mashالأعمش
  5. 05Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry107/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 286
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahman narrated to us, from Mujahid, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr, who said:

Matn

“Whoever says when he rises from his gathering: Glory be to You, O God, and with Your praise; I bear witness that there is no god but God; I seek Your forgiveness and repent to You, God expiates for him every sin in that gathering.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حُصَيْنُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو قَالَ: «

Matn

مَنْ قَالَ حِينَ يَقُومُ مِنْ مَجْلِسِهِ: سُبْحَانَكَ اللَّهُمَّ وَبِحَمْدِكَ، أَشْهَدُ أَنْ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، أَسْتَغْفِرُكَ وَأَتُوبُ إِلَيْكَ ، إِلَّا كَفَّرَ اللَّهُ عَنْهُ كُلَّ ذَنْبٍ فِي ذَلِكَ الْمَجْلِسِ»

Transmission path

  2. 02Mujahidمجاهد

Entry108/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 287
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Dawud b. Abi Hind narrated to us, from Ibn Sirin, from Abu Hurayra, who said:

Matn

“God has one hundred names less one; whoever enumerates them enters Paradise.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا دَاوُدُ بْنُ أَبِي هِنْدَ، عَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ لِلَّهِ مِائَةَ اسْمٍ غَيْرَ وَاحِدٍ ، مَنْ أَحْصَاهَا دَخَلَ الْجَنَّةَ»

  2. 02Ibn Sirinابن سيرين
  3. 03Dawud b. Abi Hindداود بن ابي هند

Entry109/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 288
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Muhammad b. 'Ubayd Allah narrated to us, from 'Amr b. Shu'ayb, from his father, from his grandfather, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Take your shield, take your shield” (meaning weapons) “against the Fire.” They said: “O Messenger of God, from an enemy at hand?” He said: “No, but: Glory be to God, praise be to God, there is no god but God, and God is greatest, for they have those that follow, those that protect, those that go before, and those that come after, and they are the enduring righteous deeds.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَدِّهِ، قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

خُذُوا جُنَّتَكُمْ، خُذُوا جُنَّتَكُمْ - يَعْنِي السِّلَاحَ - مِنَ النَّارِ» . قَالُوا: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَمِنْ عَدُوٍّ حَضَرَ ؟ قَالَ: «لَا ، وَلَكِنْ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ ، فَإِنَّ لَهُنَّ مُعَقِّبَاتٍ وَمُجَنِّبَاتٍ، وَمُقَدِّمَاتٍ وَمُؤَخِّرَاتٍ، وَهُنَّ الْبَاقِيَاتُ الصَّالِحَاتُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'AmrEarliest transmitterعبد الله بن عمرو
  2. 02Shu'ayb b. Muhammadشعيب بن محمد
  3. 03'Amr b. Shu'aybعمرو بن شعيب
  4. 04Muhammad b. 'Ubayd Allahمحمد بن عبيد الله
  5. 05Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry110/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 290
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Asim al-Ahwal narrated to us, from Bakr b. 'Abd Allah al-Muzani, who said:

Matn

It used to be said: “One of the coverings between the nakedness of the children of Adam and the eyes of the jinn and the devils is that one of you say, when he takes off his clothes: In the name of God.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَاصِمٌ الْأَحْوَلُ، عَنْ بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الْمُزَنِيِّ قَالَ: كَانَ يُقَالُ: «

Matn

إِنَّ مِنْ سِتْرِ مَا بَيْنَ عَوْرَاتِ بَنِي آدَمَ وَبَيْنَ أَعْيُنِ الْجِنِّ وَالشَّيَاطِينِ أَنْ يَقُولَ أَحَدُكُمْ إِذَا وَضَعَ ثِيَابَهُ: بِسْمِ اللَّهِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Bakr b. 'Abd Allah al-MuzaniEarliest transmitterبكر بن عبد الله المزني
  2. 02'Asim al-Ahwalعاصم الاحول
  3. 03Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry111/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 291
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahman narrated to us, from Isma'il, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said:

Matn

Abu Sa'id, when he finished a meal, would say: “Praise be to God who has fed us and given us drink and made us Muslims.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حُصَيْنُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ أَبُو سَعِيدٍ إِذَا فَرَغَ مِنْ طَعَامٍ قَالَ: «الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي أَطْعَمَنَا وَسَقَانَا، وَجَعَلَنَا مُسْلِمِينَ»

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu Sa'id al-KhudriEarliest transmitterابي سعيد الخدري
  2. 02Isma'ilاسماعيل
  3. 03Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahmanحصين بن عبد الرحمن
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry112/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 293
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-Ajlah narrated to us, from 'Abd Allah b. Abi al-Hudhayl, who said:

Matn

“God loves to be remembered in the markets, because of the people's clamor and heedlessness; and I go to the market having no need in it except to remember God.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَجْلَحُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي الْهُذَيْلِ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ اللَّهَ لَيُحِبُّ أَنْ يُذْكَرَ فِي الْأَسْوَاقِ، وَذَلِكَ لِلَغَطِ النَّاسِ ، وَغَفْلَتِهِمْ، وَإِنِّي لَآتِي السُّوقَ وَمَا لِي فِيهِ حَاجَةٌ إِلَّا أَنْ أَذْكُرَ اللَّهَ»

  2. 02al-Ajlahالاجلح
  3. 03Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry113/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 293-294
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Sabit, who said:

Matn

“Light up your houses with the remembrance of God; give your houses a share of your prayer; and do not make them graves as the Jews and Christians made their houses graves; for the house in which the Qur'an is recited shines for the people of heaven as the stars shine for the people of earth.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ سَابِطٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

أَنِيرُوا بُيُوتَكُمْ بِذِكْرِ اللَّهِ، وَاجْعَلُوا لِبُيُوتِكُمْ مِنْ صَلَاتِكُمْ جُزْءًا، وَلَا تَتَّخِذُوهَا قَبْرًا كَمَا اتَّخَذَتِ الْيَهُودُ وَالنَّصَارَى بُيُوتَهُمْ قُبُورًا، فَإِنَّ الْبَيْتَ الَّذِي يُقْرَأُ فِيهِ الْقُرْآنُ يُضِيءُ لِأَهْلِ السَّمَاءِ كَمَا يُضِيءُ النُّجُومُ لِأَهْلِ الْأَرْضِ»

Entry114/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 295
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Ibrahim al-Hajari narrated to us, from al-Walid b. 'Utba, who said: 'Ali said:

Matn

“The son of Adam has seven locks: if he speaks to himself of a good deed and brings it out through all the locks, ten are written for him; if he does not bring it out through the locks, one good deed is written. If he speaks to himself of an evil deed and brings it out through all the locks, one evil deed is written; if he does not bring it out through all the locks, nothing is written against him.” They said: “O Abu al-Hasan, what are these locks?” He said: “The heart is a lock, the tongue is a lock, the uvula is a lock, the two jaws are a lock, and the two lips are a lock.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ الْهَجَرِيُّ، عَنِ الْوَلِيدِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ، قَالَ عَلِيٌّ ﵁: «

Matn

عَلَى ابْنِ آدَمَ سَبْعَةُ أَغْلَاقٍ فَإِنْ حَدَّثَ نَفْسَهُ بِحَسَنَةٍ فَأَخْرَجَهَا مِنَ الْأَغْلَاقِ كُلِّهَا كُتِبَتْ لَهُ عَشْرًا، وَإِنْ لَمْ يُخْرِجْهَا مِنَ الْأَغْلَاقِ كُتِبَتْ حَسَنَةً، وَإِنْ حَدَّثَ نَفْسَهُ بِسَيِّئَةٍ فَأَخْرَجَهَا مِنَ الْأَغْلَاقِ كُلِّهَا كُتِبَتْ سَيِّئَةً، وَإِنْ لَمْ يُخْرِجْهَا مِنَ الْأَغْلَاقِ كُلِّهَا لَمْ يُكْتُبْ عَلَيْهِ شَيْءٌ» . فَقَالُوا: يَا أَبَا الْحَسَنِ، مَا هَذِهِ الْأَغْلَاقُ؟ قَالَ: «الْقَلْبُ غَلَقٌ، وَاللِّسَانُ غَلَقٌ، وَاللَّهَاةُ غَلَقٌ، وَاللِّحْيَانِ غَلَقٌ وَالشَّفَتَانِ غَلَقٌ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'AliEarliest transmitterعلي
  2. 02al-Walid b. 'Utbaالوليد بن عتبة
  3. 03Ibrahim al-Hajariإبراهيم الهجري
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry115/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 295
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Dawud narrated to us, from 'Amir, who said: 'Abd Allah [b. Mas'ud] said:

Matn

“Four [things], after which none perishes but one doomed to perish.” He said: “When a man does a good deed, ten are written; if he intends it but does not do it, one good deed is written; if he does an evil deed, one evil deed is written; and if he does not do it but intended it, nothing is written against him.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا دَاوُدُ، عَنْ عَامِرٍ قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ: «

Matn

أَرْبَعٌ لَا يَهْلِكُ بَعْدَهُنَّ إِلَّا هَالِكٌ ، قَالَ: إِذَا عَمِلَ الرَّجُلُ الْحَسَنَةَ كُتِبَتْ عَشْرًا، وَإِنْ هَمَّ بِهَا وَلَمْ يَعْمَلْهَا كُتِبَتْ حَسَنَةً، وَإِنْ عَمِلَ سَيِّئَةً كُتِبَتْ سَيِّئَةً، وَإِنْ لَمْ يَعْمَلْهَا وَهَمَّ بِهَا لَمْ يُكْتَبْ عَلَيْهِ شَيْءٌ»

Transmission path

  2. 02'Amirعامر
  3. 03Dawudداود

Entry116/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 296
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from al-Minhal b. 'Amr, from Muhammad b. 'Ali, who said:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ used to seek protection for al-Hasan and al-Husayn, saying: “I commend you both to the perfect word of God, from every devil and vermin, and from every evil eye.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنِ الْمِنْهَالِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَلِيٍّ قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ يُعَوِّذُ حَسَنًا وَحُسَيْنًا يَقُولُ: «أُعِيذُكُمَا بِكَلِمَةِ اللَّهِ التَّامَّةِ ، مِنْ كُلِّ شَيْطَانٍ وَهَامَّةٍ، وَمِنْ كُلِّ عَيْنٍ لَامَّةٍ»

  2. 02al-Minhal b. 'Amrالمنهال بن عمرو

Entry117/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 297-298
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahman narrated to us, from Hilal b. Yisaf, from Suhaym b. Nawfal, who said:

Matn

While we were with 'Abd Allah, a slave girl came to her master and said: “What keeps you sitting? Get up and find a healer, for so-and-so has struck your horse [with the evil eye] and left it circling as if it were a spindle.” 'Abd Allah said: “Do not seek a healer, but go to it and spit into its right nostril four times and into the left three times, and say: In the name of God; no harm; remove the harm, O Lord of the people, and heal, for You are the Healer; none removes harm but You.” He said: We had not risen from 'Abd Allah before he [the owner] came and said: “I said what you said, and it did not leave the place until it ate, drank, defecated and urinated.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حُصَيْنُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ هِلَالِ بْنِ يَسَافٍ، عَنْ سُحَيْمِ بْنِ نَوْفَلٍ قَالَ:

Matn

بَيْنَا نَحْنُ عِنْدَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ إِذْ جَاءَتْ جَارِيَةٌ إِلَى سَيِّدِهَا ، وَقَالَتْ: مَا يُقْعِدُكَ؟ قُمْ فَابْتَغِ رَاقِيًا، فَإِنَّ فُلَانًا قَدْ لَقَعَ فَرَسَكَ، فَتَرَكَهُ يَدُورُ كَأَنَّهُ فَلَكٌ، فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ: «لَا تَبْتَغِ رَاقِيًا، وَلَكِنِ ائْتِهِ فَاتْفُلْ فِي مَنْخَرِهِ الْأَيْمَنِ أَرْبَعًا، وَفِي الْأَيْسَرِ ثَلَاثًا، وَقُلْ: بِسْمِ اللَّهِ ، لَا بَأْسَ، أَذْهِبِ الْبَأْسَ ، رَبَّ النَّاسِ، وَاشْفِ ، أَنْتَ الشَّافِي ، لَا يَكْشِفُ الضُّرَّ إِلَّا أَنْتَ» . قَالَ: فَمَا قُمْنَا مِنْ عِنْدِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ حَتَّى جَاءَ فَقَالَ: قُلْتُ الَّذِي قُلْتَ، فَلَمْ أَبْرَحْ حَتَّى أَكَلَ وَشَرِبَ وَرَاثَ وَبَالَ

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Suhaym b. NawfalEarliest transmitterسحيم بن نوفل
  2. 02Hilal b. Yisafهلال بن يساف
  3. 03Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahmanحصين بن عبد الرحمن
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry118/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 299-300
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Ata' b. al-Sa'ib narrated to us, from Abu 'Abd al-Rahman, from Ibn Mas'ud, from the Prophet ﷺ, that he used to say:

Matn

“O God, I seek refuge in You from Satan: from his hamz, his nafkh and his nafth.” He said: “His hamz is death, his nafth is poetry, and his nafkh is arrogance.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَطَاءُ بْنُ السَّائِبِ، عَنْ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ: «

Matn

اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ: مِنْ هَمْزِهِ، وَنَفْخِهِ، وَنَفْثِهِ» . قَالَ: «فَهَمْزُهُ: الْمَوْتُ ، وَنَفْثُهُ: الشِّعْرُ، وَنَفْخُهُ: الْكِبْرُ»

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Ibn Mas'udEarliest transmitterابن مسعود
  2. 02Abu 'Abd al-Rahmanأبو عبد الرحمن
  3. 03'Ata' b. al-Sa'ibعطاء بن السائب
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry119/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 301-302
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Abu Ishaq al-Shaybani narrated to us, from Yasir b. 'Amr, who said:

Matn

Ghouls were mentioned in the presence of 'Umar, and he said: “There is nothing able to change from the creation God created it in, but they have sorcerers like your sorcerers; so if you see anything of that, give the call to prayer.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو إِسْحَاقَ الشَّيْبَانِيُّ، عَنْ يُسَيْرِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو قَالَ:

Matn

ذُكِرَتِ الْغِيلَانُ عِنْدَ عُمَرَ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّهُ لَيْسَ مِنْ شَيْءٍ يَسْتَطِيعُ يَتَغَيَّرُ عَنْ خَلْقِ اللَّهِ الَّذِي خَلَقَهُ، وَلَكِنَّ لَهُمْ سَحَرَةً كَسَحَرَتِكُمْ، فَإِذَا رَأَيْتُمْ مِنْ ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا ، فَأَذِّنُوا»

English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-'Ala' b. al-Musayyab narrated to us, from al-Fudayl b. 'Amr, who said:

Matn

A man came to 'Ali and said: “So-and-so is sick.” He said: “Would it please you for him to recover?” He said: “Say: O God, O Forbearing, O Generous, heal so-and-so.”

العربية
Isnad

Matn

فَيَسُرُّكَ أَنْ يَبْرَأَ» ؟ قَالَ: «قُلِ: اللَّهُمَّ يَا حَلِيمُ ، يَا كَرِيمُ ، اشْفِ فُلَانًا»

Entry121/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 305-306
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, from Mujahid, from Tubay', who said: Ka'b said:

Matn

“Were it not for some words that I say, the Jews would make me cry out with the braying donkeys and howl with the howling dogs: I seek refuge in Your noble Face, Your mighty Name, and Your perfect words that neither the righteous nor the wicked can transgress, [Your Name] that does not fail its neighbor, from the evil of what descends from the sky, the evil of what ascends in it, and the evil of what He created, spread and made.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، عَنْ تُبَيْعٍ، قَالَ كَعْبٌ: «

Matn

لَوْلَا كَلِمَاتٌ أَقُولُهُنَّ لَجَعَلَتْنِي يَهُودُ أَصِيحُ مَعَ الْحَمِيرِ النَاهِقَةِ، وَأَعْوِي مَعَ الْكِلَابِ الْعَاوِيَةِ: أَعُوذُ بِوَجْهِكَ الْكَرِيمِ ، وَبِاسْمِكَ الْعَظِيمِ ، وَبِكَلِمَاتِكَ التَّامَّاتِ ، الَّتِي لَا يُجَاوِزُهُنَّ بَرٌّ وَلَا فَاجِرٌ ، الَّذِي لَا يُخْفَرُ جَارُهُ ، مِنْ شَرِّ مَا يَنْزِلُ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ، وَمَنْ شَرِّ مَا يَعْرُجُ فِيهَا، وَمَنْ شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ وَذَرَأَ وَبَرَأَ»

  2. 02Tubay'تبيع
  3. 03Mujahidمجاهد
  4. 04Laythليث
  5. 05Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry122/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 306-307
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; 'Abd al-Rahman b. Ishaq narrated to us, from al-Qasim b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from Ibn Mas'ud, who said:

Matn

“No servant wakes in the night and says: 'There is no god but You, my Lord; I have wronged myself, so forgive me,' but he comes out of his sins as the snake comes out of its skin.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «

Matn

مَا تَعَارَّ عَبْدٌ مِنَ اللَّيْلِ ، فَقَالَ: لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا أَنْتَ ، رَبِّ ، ظَلَمْتُ نَفْسِي ، فَاغْفِرْ لِي، إِلَّا خَرَجَ مِنْ ذُنُوبِهِ كَمَا تَخْرُجُ الْحَيَّةُ مِنْ سَلْخِهَا»

Entry123/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 310
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Ibn Marzuq narrated to us, from 'Atiyya, from Abu Sa'id, who said:

Matn

“When a man wakes and says: 'Glory be to God who gives life to the dead, and He has power over all things,' God says: My servant has spoken truly and given thanks.” He said: And he says with that: “O God, forgive me my sin on the day You raise me from my grave; O God, protect me from Your punishment on the day You raise Your servants.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ مَرْزُوقٍ، عَنْ عَطِيَّةَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِذَا اسْتَيْقَظَ الرَّجُلُ فَقَالَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ الَّذِي يُحْيِي الْمَوْتَى، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ. قَالَ اللَّهُ: صَدَقَ عَبْدِي وَشَكَرَ» . قَالَ: وَيَقُولُ عِنْدَ ذَلِكَ: «اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي ذَنْبِي يَوْمَ تَبْعَثُنِي مِنْ قَبْرِي، اللَّهُمَّ قِنِي عَذَابَكَ يَوْمَ تَبْعَثُ عِبَادَكَ»

Transmission path

  2. 02'Atiyyaعطية

Entry124/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 311
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Husayn b. 'Abd al-Rahman narrated to us, from Mujahid, who said:

Matn

I set out, I and Yahya b. Ja'da, and we entered upon a man of the Ansar called 'Abd al-Rahman b. Abi 'Amra, who was speaking to the people. He said: “When it is the depth of the night, or the last part of the night, an angel looks down from the sky and says: Glory be to the Holy King. Then another looks down and says: Glory be to the Holy King; that is when the birds spread their wings. Then another looks down and says: O seeker of good, come forward. Then another looks down and says: O seeker of evil, hold back. Then another looks down and says: O God, give a replacement to every one who spends. Then another looks down and says: O God, give ruin to every one who withholds.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حُصَيْنُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ قَالَ:

Matn

انْطَلَقْتُ أَنَا وَيَحْيَى بْنُ جَعْدَةَ، فَدَخَلْنَا عَلَى رَجُلٍ مِنَ الْأَنْصَارِ يُقَالُ لَهُ: عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ أَبِي عَمْرَةَ ، وَهُوَ يُحَدِّثُ الْقَوْمَ قَالَ: «إِذَا كَانَ جَوْفُ اللَّيْلِ - أَوْ مِنْ آخِرِ اللَّيْلِ - اطَّلَعَ مَلَكٌ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ فَقَالَ: سُبْحَانَ الْمَلِكِ الْقُدُّوسِ، ثُمَّ اطَّلَعَ آخَرُ فَقَالَ: سُبْحَانَ الْمَلِكِ الْقُدُّوسِ، فَذَلِكَ حِينَ تُخْرِجُ الطَّيْرُ أَجْنِحَتَهَا، ثُمَّ يَطَّلِعُ آخَرُ فَيَقُولُ: يَا بَاغِيَ الْخَيْرِ أَقْبِلْ، ثُمَّ يَطَّلِعُ آخَرُ فَيَقُولُ: يَا بَاغِيَ الشَّرِّ أَقْصِرْ، ثُمَّ يَطَّلِعُ آخَرُ فَيَقُولُ: اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْ لِكُلِّ مُنْفِقٍ خَلَفًا، ثُمَّ يَطَّلِعُ آخَرُ فَيَقُولُ: اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْ لِكُلِّ مُمْسِكٍ تَلَفًا»

Transmission path

  2. 02Mujahidمجاهد

Entry125/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 311-312
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Abu Hamza al-Thumali narrated to us, from Salim b. Abi al-Ja'd, who said:

Matn

“Among what God created is a rooster whose feet are in the lowest earth and whose wings are in the air, and whose neck is bent below the Throne. Every night he calls: 'Glory be to the Holy King'; then he says: 'Most Glorious, Most Holy, Lord of the angels and the Spirit; our Lord is God, the King, the Merciful, there is no god but Him'; and at that the hens flap their wings.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو حَمْزَةَ الثُّمَالِيُّ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ أَبِي الْجَعْدِ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ مِمَّا خَلَقَ اللَّهُ لَدِيكًا ، رِجْلَاهُ فِي الْأَرْضِ السُّفْلَى، وَجَنَاحَاهُ فِي الْهَوَاءِ، وَعُنُقُهُ مَثْنِيَّةٌ دُونَ الْعَرْشِ ، يُنَادِي كُلَّ لَيْلَةٍ: سُبْحَانَ الْمَلِكِ الْقُدُّوسِ، ثُمَّ يَقُولُ: سُبُّوحٌ قُدُّوسٌ، رَبُّ الْمَلَائِكَةِ وَالرُّوحِ ، رَبُّنَا اللَّهُ، الْمَلِكُ الرَّحْمَنُ ، لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا هُوَ، وَعِنْدَهُ تُحَرِّكُ الدَّجَاجُ أَجْنِحَتَهَا»

Transmission path

  1. 01Salim b. Abi al-Ja'dEarliest transmitterسالم بن أبي الجعد
  2. 02Abu Hamza al-Thumaliأبو حمزة الثمالي
  3. 03Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry126/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 314-315
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Mis'ar narrated to us, from 'Alqama b. Marthad, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Sabit, who said:

Matn

Khalid b. al-Walid suffered from sleeplessness, and the Prophet ﷺ said: “Shall I not teach you words which, when you say them, you will sleep?” He said: “Yes, O Messenger of God.” He said: “Say: O God, Lord of the seven heavens and what they shade, and the seven earths and what they bear, and Lord of the devils and whom they lead astray, be a protector for me against the evil of all Your creation, lest any of them transgress against me or oppress me; mighty is Your protection, and majestic is Your praise, and there is no god other than You.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مِسْعَرٌ، عَنْ عَلْقَمَةَ بْنِ مَرْثَدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ سَابِطٍ قَالَ:

Matn

أَصَابَ خَالِدَ بْنَ الْوَلِيدِ أَرَقٌ ، فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «أَلَا أُعَلِّمُكَ كَلِمَاتٍ إِذَا قُلْتَهُنَّ نِمْتَ؟» قَالَ: بَلَى يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ. قَالَ: «قُلِ: اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّ السَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعِ وَمَا أَظَلَّتْ، وَالْأَرَضِينَ السَّبْعِ وَمَا أَقَلَّتْ، وَرَبَّ الشَّيَاطِينِ وَمَا أَضَلَّتْ، كُنْ لِي جَارًا مِنْ شَرِّ خَلْقِكَ جَمِيعًا كُلِّهِمْ، أنْ يَفْرُطَ عَلَيَّ أَحَدٌ مِنْهُمْ، أَوْ يَبْغِيَ، عَزَّ جَارُكَ، وَجَلَّ ثَنَاؤُكَ، وَلَا إِلَهَ غَيْرُكَ»

Transmission path

  2. 02'Alqama b. Marthadعلقمة بن مرثد
  3. 03Mis'arمسعر
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry127/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 316
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Ibrahim al-Hajari narrated to us, from Abu al-Ahwas, from 'Abd Allah [b. Mas'ud], who said: The Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“God opens the doors of heaven for the remaining third [of the night], then descends to the lowest heaven and stretches out His hand: Is there no servant who asks Me, that I may give him? And He remains thus until dawn breaks.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ الْهَجَرِيُّ، عَنْ أَبِي الْأَحْوَصِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

إِنَّ اللَّهَ لَيَفْتَحُ أَبْوَابَ السَّمَاءِ للثُّلِثِ الْبَاقِي، ثُمَّ يَهْبِطُ إِلَى السَّمَاءِ الدُّنْيَا، ثُمَّ يَبْسُطُ يَدَهُ: أَلَا عَبْدٌ يَسْأَلُنِي أُعْطِيَهُ، فَمَا يَزَالُ كَذَلِكَ حَتَّى يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ»

  2. 02Abu al-Ahwasأبو الأحوص

Entry128/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 317
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Hamza al-Zayyat narrated to us, from Ziyad al-Ta'i, from Abu Hurayra, who said:

Matn

We said: “O Messenger of God, what is it with us that when we are with you our hearts soften, we renounce the world and are of the people of the Hereafter, but when we leave you we find in ourselves what we dislike, embrace our wives and smell our children?” He said: “If you were, when you left me, in the state you are in with me, the angels would visit you in your houses; and if you did not sin, God would bring a new creation that would sin, and He would forgive them.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حَمْزَةُ الزَّيَّاتُ، عَنْ زِيَادٍ الطَّائِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ قَالَ:

Matn

قُلْنَا: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مَا لَنَا إِذَا كُنَّا عِنْدَكَ رَقَّتْ قُلُوبُنَا ، وَزَهِدْنَا فِي الدُّنْيَا، وَكُنَّا مِنْ أَهْلِ الْآخِرَةِ، وَإِذَا خَرَجْنَا مِنْ عِنْدِكَ رَأَيْنَا مِنْ أَنْفُسِنَا مَا نَكْرَهُ، فَعَانَقْنَا أَهْلَنَا، وَشَمَمْنَا أَوْلَادَنَا؟ فَقَالَ: «لَوْ أَنَّكُمْ تَكُونُونَ إِذَا خَرَجْتُمْ مِنْ عِنْدِي عَلَى الْحَالِ الَّذِي أَنْتُمْ عَلَيْهَا عِنْدِي ، لَزَارَتْكُمُ الْمَلَائِكَةُ فِي بُيُوتِكُمْ، وَلَوْ أَنَّكُمْ لَا تُذْنِبُونَ لَجَاءَ اللَّهُ بِخَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ يُذْنِبُونَ، فَيَغْفِرُ لَهُمْ»

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu HurayraEarliest transmitterأبو هريرة
  2. 02Ziyad al-Ta'iزياد الطائي
  3. 03Hamza al-Zayyatحمزة الزيات
  4. 04Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 318
English
Isnad

No chain of its own; continues the preceding report.

Matn

He said: We said: “O Messenger of God, from what was creation created?” He said: “From water.”

العربية
Isnad

No isnad: the text is given as a direct statement.

Matn

قَالَ: قُلْنَا: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مِمَّا خُلِقَ الْخَلْقُ؟ قَالَ: «مِنَ الْمَاءِ»

Entry128c/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 318
English
Isnad

No chain of its own; continues the preceding report.

Matn

He said: We said: “O Messenger of God, tell us about Paradise: what is its construction?” He said: “A brick of silver and a brick of gold; its mortar is fragrant musk; its pebbles are pearls and rubies; its soil is saffron. Whoever enters it will live forever and not die, will enjoy comfort and not suffer, his clothes will not wear out, and his youth will not fade.”

العربية
Isnad

No isnad: the text is given as a direct statement.

Matn

قَالَ: قُلْنَا: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَخْبِرْنَا عَنِ الْجَنَّةِ: مَا بِنَاؤُهَا؟ قَالَ: «لَبِنَةٌ مِنْ فِضَّةٍ، وَلَبِنَةٌ مِنْ ذَهَبٍ، وَمِلَاطُهَا الْمِسْكُ الْأَذْفَرُ، وَحَصْبَاؤُهَا اللُّؤْلُؤُ وَالْيَاقُوتُ، وَتُرْبَتُهَا الزَّعْفَرَانُ، مَنْ يَدْخُلُهَا يَخْلُدُ ، وَلَا يَموُتُ، وَيَنْعَمُ لَا يَبْؤُسُ ، لَا تَبْلَى ثِيَابُهُ، وَلَا يَفْنَى شَبَابُهُ»

  2. 02Ziyad al-Ta'iزياد الطائي

Entry128d/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 318
English
Isnad

No chain of its own; continues the preceding report.

Matn

Then he said: “Three whose supplication is not rejected: the just ruler; the fasting person until he breaks his fast; and the supplication of the wronged, which is raised above the clouds, and the Lord looks at it and says: By My might and majesty, I will help you, even if after a while.”

العربية
Isnad

No isnad: the text is given as a direct statement.

Matn

ثُمَّ قَالَ: «ثَلَاثَةٌ لَا يُرَدُّ دُعَاؤُهُمْ: إِمَامٌ عَادِلٌ، وَالصَّائِمُ حَتَّى يُفْطِرَ، وَدَعْوَةُ الْمَظْلُومِ، فَإِنَّهَا تُرْفَعُ فَوْقَ الْغَمَامِ، فَيَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهَا الرَّبُّ ، فَيَقُولُ: وَعِزَّتِي وَجَلَالِي ، لَأَنْصُرَنَّكِ وَلَوْ بَعْدَ حِينٍ»

Entry129/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 320
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth narrated to us, raising it to the Prophet ﷺ:

Matn

“O God, I seek refuge in You from the two collapses and the two blind [calamities], and from qatra and what it begot.” Muhammad said: “The two collapses: the falling [of a wall] and the well; the two blind: the flood and the sea; and qatra is the first snake.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ يَرْفَعُهُ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ ﷺ ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ: «اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنَ الْهَدْمَتَيْنِ وَالْعَمَيَيْنِ، وَمِنْ قِتْرَةٍ وَمَا وَلَدَ» قَالَ مُحَمَّدٌ: «الْهَدْمَتَيْنِ: الْخَبَطُ وَالْبِئْرُ، وَالْأَعْمَيَيْنِ: السَّيْلُ وَالْبَحْرُ، وَقِتْرَةٌ الْحَيَّةُ الْأُولَى»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01LaythEarliest transmitterليث
  2. 02Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي
Source flags
  • Raised by layth without intermediate authority
  • Gloss by Muhammad b Fudayl included
Remark attached in the source: Editorial or transmitter note
The gloss on the three terms is by the compiler, Muhammad b. Fudayl.

Entry130/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 321-322
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Layth b. Abi Sulaym and Musa Abu Ja'far al-Thaqafi narrated to us, from Shahr b. Hawshab, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Ghanm, from Abu Dharr, from the Prophet ﷺ, who said:

Matn

“God says: O My servants, all of you are sinners except those I have kept safe, so ask My forgiveness and I will forgive you. Whoever of you knows that I have power to forgive and asks Me by My power, I forgive him and do not mind. All of you are astray except those I have guided, so call on Me and I will guide you. All of you are poor except those I have enriched, so ask Me and I will provide for you. If the living and the dead among you, the first and the last, the moist and the dry, were to gather upon the most wretched heart of one of My servants, that would not diminish My dominion by the wing of a gnat; and if they gathered upon the most pious heart of one of My servants, that would not add to My dominion the wing of a gnat. And if the living and the dead among you, the first and the last, the moist and the dry, were to gather and ask Me, each asker of them reaching his wish, I would give each asker what he asked of Me, and that would diminish Me only as if one of you stood on the shore of the sea, dipped a needle in it and drew it out. That is because I am Generous, Giving, Glorious, Finding; I do what I will. My gift is speech, My punishment is speech, and My command to a thing, when I want it, is only that I say 'Be,' and it is.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثُ بْنُ أَبِي سُلَيْمٍ، وَمُوسَى أَبُو جَعْفَرٍ الثَّقَفِيُّ، عَنْ شَهْرِ بْنِ حَوْشَبٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ غَنْمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ قَالَ: «

Matn

يَقُولُ اللَّهُ: يَا عِبَادِي ، كُلُّكُمْ مُذْنِبٌ إِلَّا مَنْ عَافَيْتُ ، فَاسْتَغْفِرُونِي أَغْفِرْ لَكُمْ، مَنْ عَلِمَ مِنْكُمْ أَنِّي ذُو قُدْرَةٍ عَلَى الْمَغْفِرَةِ ، فَسَأَلَنِي بِقُدْرَتِي غَفَرْتُ لَهُ ، وَلَا أُبَالِي، وَكُلُّكُمْ ضَالٌّ إِلَّا مَنْ هَدَيْتُ، فَادْعُونِي أَهْدِكُمْ، وَكُلُّكُمْ فَقِيرٌ إِلَّا مَنْ أَغْنَيْتُ ، فَسَلُونِي أَرْزُقْكُمْ، وَلَوْ أَنَّ حَيَّكُمْ وَمَيِّتَكُمْ، وَأَوَّلَكُمْ وَآخِرَكُمْ، وَرَطْبَكُمْ وَيَابِسَكُمُ اجْتَمَعُوا عَلَى أَشْقَى قَلْبِ عَبْدٍ مِنْ عِبَادِي ، لَمْ يَنْقُصْ ذَلِكَ مِنْ مُلْكِي جَنَاحَ بَعُوضَةٍ، وَلَوِ اجْتَمَعُوا عَلَى أَتْقَى قَلْبِ عَبْدٍ مِنْ عِبَادِي لَمْ يَزِدْ ذَلِكَ فِي مُلْكِي جَنَاحَ بَعُوضَةٍ، وَلَوْ أَنَّ حَيَّكُمْ وَمَيِّتَكُمْ، وَأَوَّلَكُمْ وَآخِرَكُمْ، وَرَطْبَكُمْ وَيَابِسَكُمُ اجْتَمَعُوا فَسَأَلُونِي ، كُلُّ سَائِلٍ مِنْهُمْ مَا بَلَغَتْ أُمْنِيَّتُهُ ، لَأَعْطَيْتُ كُلَّ سَائِلٍ مِنْهُمْ مَا سَأَلَنِي، مَا نَقَصَنِي ذَلِكَ إِلَّا كَمَا لَوْ أَنَّ أَحَدَكُمْ عَلَى شَفَةِ الْبَحْرِ، فَغَمَسَ فِيهِ إِبْرَةً ، ثُمَّ انْتَزَعَهَا، وَذَلِكَ أَنِّي جَوَادٌ كَرِيمٌ، مَاجِدٌ وَاجِدٌ، أَفْعَلُ مَا أَشَاءُ ، عَطَائِي كَلَامٌ، وَعَذَابِي كَلَامٌ، وَأَمْرِي لِلشَّيْءِ إِذَا أَرَدْتُهُ أَنْ أَقُولَ: كُنْ ، فَيَكُونَ»

Entry131/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 323
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; Fitr b. Khalifa narrated to us, from Abu Ishaq, from 'Ubayd b. al-Mughira, who said: I heard Hudhayfa say: I said:

Matn

“O Messenger of God, there is a sharpness in my tongue toward my family.” He said: “Where are you from seeking forgiveness? I seek God's forgiveness so many times a day.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا فِطْرُ بْنُ خَلِيفَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ بْنِ الْمُغِيرَةِ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ حُذَيْفَةَ، يَقُولُ: قُلْتُ:

Matn

يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ، إِنَّ فِي لِسَانِي ذَرَبًا عَلَى أَهْلِي ، قَالَ: «فَأَيْنَ أَنْتَ مِنَ الِاسْتِغْفَارِ؟ إِنِّي لَأَسْتَغْفِرُ اللَّهَ فِي الْيَوْمِ كَذَا وَكَذَا»

  2. 02'Ubayd b. al-Mughiraعبيد بن المغيرة
  3. 03Abu Ishaqأبو إسحاق
  4. 04Fitr b. Khalifaفطر بن خليفة
  5. 05Muhammad b. Fudayl b. Ghazwan al-DabbiCompilerمحمد بن فضيل بن غزوان الضبي

Entry132/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 325
English
Isnad

Ibn Fudayl narrated to us; al-A'mash narrated to us, from 'Umara b. 'Umayr, from al-Harith b. Suwayd, who said: I came to 'Abd Allah b. Mas'ud to visit him, and he told me a hadith from the Prophet ﷺ and a hadith from himself. He said:

Matn

“The believer sees his sins as if he were at the foot of a mountain, fearing it will fall upon him; and the wicked sees his sins as if a fly passed over his nose,” and he did thus with his hand.

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ فُضَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ عُمَارَةَ بْنِ عُمَيْرٍ، عَنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ سُوَيْدٍ قَالَ: أَتَيْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ مَسْعُودٍ أَعُودُهُ، فَحَدَّثَنِي حَدِيثًا عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ، وَحَدِيثًا عَنْ نَفْسِهِ ، قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ الْمُؤْمِنَ يَرَى ذُنُوبَهُ كَأَنَّهُ فِي أَصْلِ جَبَلٍ ، يَخَافُ أَنْ يَنْقَلِبَ عَلَيْهِ، وَالْفَاجِرَ يَرَى ذُنُوبَهُ كَأَنَّهُ مَرَّ عَلَى أَنْفِهِ ذُبَابٌ» فَقَالَ بِهِ هَكَذَا

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. Mas'udEarliest transmitterعبد الله بن مسعود
  2. 02al-Harith b. Suwaydالحارث بن سويد

  4. 04al-A'mashالأعمش

Entry132b/ 166
Al-Duʿāʾ of Ibn Fuḍayl · p. 325-326
English
Isnad

No chain of its own; continues the preceding report.

Matn

Or he said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “God is more delighted by the repentance of His servant than a man in a perilous desert with his mount, on which are his food and drink: he lays his head down and sleeps, then wakes and his mount has gone with his food and drink; he searches for it until, when thirst and death overtake him, he says: I will return to where my mount was and die there. He lies down and dozes, then wakes, and there is his mount with his food and drink.”

العربية
Isnad

No isnad: the text is given as a direct statement.

Matn

أَوْ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «اللَّهُ أَفْرَحُ بِتَوْبَةِ عَبْدِهِ مِنْ رَجُلٍ بِأَرْضٍ دَوِيَّةٍ مَهْلَكَةٍ ، مَعَهُ رَاحِلَتُهُ ، عَلَيْهَا طَعَامُهُ وَشَرَابُهُ، فَوَضَعَ رَأْسَهُ ، فَنَامَ فَانْتَبَهَ، وَقَدْ ذَهَبَتْ رَاحِلَتُهُ عَلَيْهَا طَعَامُهُ وَشَرَابُهُ، فَالْتَمَسَهَا ، حَتَّى إِذَا أَدْرَكَهُ الْعَطَشُ وَالْمَوْتُ قَالَ: أَرْجِعُ إِلَى مَوْضِعِ رَاحِلَتِي وَأَمُوتُ فِيهِ ، قَالَ: فَاضْطَجَعَ فَأَغْفَى إِغْفَاءَةً، فَاسْتَيْقَظَ ، فَإِذَا رَاحِلَتُهُ عَلَيْهَا طَعَامُهُ وَشَرَابُهُ»

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. Mas'udEarliest transmitterعبد الله بن مسعود
  2. 02al-Harith b. Suwaydالحارث بن سويد
